# Catalog-wide source review — 25 October 2026

Reviewed the **2,561 remaining IANs** outside the previous 586-IAN Kompernass import. The catalog remains at **3,147 products**. This pass updates **1,166 records**, adds **1,573 specification fields**, corrects 8 existing fields and removes 1 unsupported field.

## Search and review coverage

- Queried the official Parkside manual service for all 2561 remaining IANs: 1990 successful cached results from 23 October were reused and 571 additional IANs were searched. Outcomes: 845 found, 1716 not found, zero errors and zero ambiguous service responses. A not-found result is limited to that service; it does not prove a manual does not exist elsewhere.
- Acquired 1645 candidate PDF files for 1169 IANs from existing catalog links, official search results and the manufacturer’s website, with no download failures. Text extraction covered every page; relevant image-only pages and critical technical/manufacturer statements received visual inspection. Font encoding was repaired in 88 documents before comparison.
- Matched IANs and product scope before using document facts. Standard technical tables were compared against each record; unclear kit, accessory and assortment data were held back or explicitly scoped to the documented component. This is a source and field comparison, not a claim that every instruction or possible specification has received an exhaustive manual transcription.
- 1391 IANs still have no PDF located in the sources checked. The table below identifies them individually. Existing descriptive data was retained; unknown fields and manufacturers were not filled by analogy.

## Applied changes

- Added 4 operating/instruction PDF links, replaced one wrong-IAN manual and removed 2 warranty/disposal-only links from the manual field.
- Recovered or corrected 6 model codes and retained all six previous public URLs. Related IAN badges and comparison tables are generated from the complete normalized model and category.
- Added 10 manufacturer attributions supported by explicit statements in each IAN’s own primary manual. Existing manufacturer confirmations and all OEM values were preserved.
- Corrected 39 power classifications using the documents. Battery and adapter specifications are kept separate; fuel and engine-oil tanks are distinct.
- Added the original embedded public JPEG for IAN 484692 from the official Lidl leaflet, page 8, together with the documented 23-piece count and 8 kg bag load capacity. No user-supplied chat photographs were used. 4 records in this review remain without a product photo.

## Material corrections

- IAN 507549: replaced the manual printed for IAN 495498 with the official Scheppach manual printed for 507549_2507. Manufacturer provenance now points to its explicit manufacturer statement. Fuel tank capacity, operating weights and engine data use this exact edition.
- IAN 541118: the radio weighs 3.1 kg without a battery in its own manual; adapter power consumption is not reported as the radio’s input power. The three radio IANs were reviewed independently.
- IAN 494762: maximum 8 bar applies specifically to PDWE 8 C3, rather than a generic 6.3 bar carried across the assortment.
- IAN 465594: 300 W continuous S1 and 400 W at S6 40% duty cycle are distinguished.
- IANs 517171 and 479242: their own covers and technical tables identify WWS-HSW1000-L01. Their documented values and provenance remain attached to their individual IANs.
- IAN 497019: the air accessory set identifies HG04394, rather than the unrelated PDSS 13 D4 model code.
- IAN 521200: new pump figures are explicitly scoped to HG10220; the assortment’s model and manufacturer remain unknown.
- IAN 488387: a drill component manual does not establish the identity or specifications of the complete seven-piece kit; no drill values were promoted to whole-kit facts.
- IANs 510795 and 459720: warranty/disposal information is retained only in optional research metadata, rather than linked as an operating manual.

## Translation handling

679 Italian translations in this reviewed group are now stale. Their existing fingerprints were preserved, so the site displays the English source with its review notice. Italian drafts were aligned for the changed manufacturer evidence and IAN 484692 title/body. Shared field labels are translated centrally. Across the full catalog, 781 existing Italian translations await human review; this includes 102 from the previous import. No translation was marked human-reviewed automatically.

## Validation

- Production build and all 47 automated tests.
- Product filename alignment and preservation of the six legacy URLs.
- Consistency/classification audit with no warnings.
- Generated-page checks for every changed record in both languages, plus browser checks for manual links, manufacturer-only identity sections, stale translation fallback, local photos, responsive layouts, automatic comparison tables and legacy redirects.

## Research artifacts

Downloaded PDFs, extracted text, preparation scripts and raw search results are kept in the ignored local research cache. The public report contains only per-IAN findings and public source URLs. Product pages contain product information and optional manuals; collection-site citations and research notes are not rendered.
